# Daily video prompt pack — 02/12/2026 · Dog day (ថ្ងៃឆ្នាំច)

Lucky signs: Dog, Tiger, Horse · Careful: Dragon
Day element: Yang Metal ✨ (Geng-xu day; ធាតុលោហៈ) · every action scene today carries Metal magic: bright silver-white sparkles, shimmering blades of light and softly chiming silver stars.
Format: vertical 9:16 (YouTube Shorts, Facebook Reels, TikTok), about 45 seconds: 4 Veo scenes of about 8 seconds, then a lucky color and direction scene of about 10–12 seconds. No lucky numbers in the video (Facebook-safe).
Two versions: make the pictures once, then record a Khmer voice-over and an English voice-over (two videos, or one video with both subtitle tracks).
Meaning scenes: in the love, work, money and horoscope scenes the zodiac guardian and its animal act out what Chanda is saying, the guardian protecting and caring and the animal trusting (no real-life people), and every scene with Chanda on camera says which feeling she shows.
Ratings, lucky signs and lucky numbers come from the site's fixed rules and match the website's daily fortune for this date.
Voice-over wording: standard script (this pack was made ahead; the site's own daily wording appears the day before).

## Today's horoscope – all 12 signs
Each sign's prediction for 02/12/2026: general luck, love, work, money and advice, in English and Khmer, ready to copy into your Facebook, TikTok, Telegram and YouTube posts. The videos speak the same predictions: the 60-second video (the Dog's luck and love), the 180-second video (love, work and money), the long videos (every sign) and the full-fortune video. Lucky numbers are given only for today's lucky signs, as in the videos.
Wording: the website's standard lines for each sign's rating (this date's written fortune is published on the website the day before; open the Studio at mybirthsign.com/studio/ then to use it).

### Khmer
🐀 ឆ្នាំជូត – ថ្ងៃធម្មតា
ទូទៅ៖ ជាថ្ងៃស្ងប់ស្ងាត់ សម្រាប់រៀបចំផែនការ។ សូមរៀបចំរបស់របរ គិតទុកមុន និងសម្រាកឲ្យបានល្អនៅយប់នេះ។
ស្នេហា៖ សូមចំណាយពេលសាមញ្ញៗជាមួយមនុស្សជិតស្និទ្ធ ដូចជាដើរលេង ឬញ៉ាំអាហារជាមួយគ្នា។
ការងារ៖ សូមរក្សាទម្លាប់ធម្មតា។ រៀបចំការងារ ពិនិត្យស្តុក ហើយបម្រើអតិថិជនម្នាក់ៗឲ្យបានល្អ។

## Luckiest animal today – Dog (ច) · Great Day
One hero post for the day's luckiest animal: a picture, a short video and a caption. Lucky color red (ក្រហម) · best time 11:00–13:00 · lucky numbers 3 · 93 · 352 · 4394 · 99343 (for fun only).

### Picture prompt (square 1:1)
Create a photorealistic hero image of @Dog (the Dog guardian with their dog), the Dog of the Chinese zodiac, as a majestic celestial guardian from a high-budget fantasy epic. The dog symbolizes loyalty, protection and honest friendship: a loyal, warm and protective expression and posture that show this, mouth calm and closed. Realistic anatomy and textures, engraved gold ornaments with physically real metallic reflections. It stands on the stone causeway of a temple inspired by Angkor Wat, between carved naga balustrades, with floating lotus lamps on the moat beside it, under an enormous luminous full moon and a deep indigo starry sky; moonbeams fall on the dog, golden particles and flowing ribbons of liquid-gold light swirl around it. Cool silver-blue moonlight, warm golden rim light, volumetric mist, cinematic depth of field, ultra-detailed. Square 1:1, full body visible, three-quarter view, centered, with space around it. No text, no letters, no watermark, no logo, no cartoon or plastic look, no cropped body parts.

A soft beam of moonlight falls on it, its golden details glow, and it rises gently into the air, floating slowly toward the moon with a trail of silver-gold sparkles and lotus petals. Loyal, warm and protective mood. Slow, smooth camera move. No text.

## 60-second video – Facebook & TikTok (8 scenes, 80 seconds)
An 80-second video about the Dog, today's luckiest animal: 8 clips of 10 seconds. Set the clip length in Flow to 10 seconds and add Chanda and the saved character @Dog as ingredients. Chanda is on camera and speaks with lip-sync in scenes 1, 8; in the other scenes she narrates off screen. The animals never speak or move their mouths: they only act. For each scene, press "Copy + English voice" or "Copy + Khmer voice": it copies the style line, the scene and the voice together. Put the clips in order 1 to 8.

### Topic and audience
Today's luckiest zodiac animal, the Dog, in 80 seconds. For viewers who follow Chinese zodiac signs, especially people born in the Year of the Dog, scrolling Facebook Reels and TikTok.

### Opening hook – 5 options
1. Identity
   English: Born in the Year of the Dog? Today is a Great Day for you. Here's your lucky color, time, and direction.
   Khmer: អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំចមែនទេ? ថ្ងៃនេះ ជាថ្ងៃល្អខ្លាំងសម្រាប់អ្នក។ មកមើលពណ៌ ពេលវេលា និងទិសសំណាងរបស់អ្នក។

   English: Out of all twelve zodiac signs, only one has a Great Day today. Is it yours?

3. Question

★ 4. Challenge
   English: Can you guess today's luckiest zodiac animal? Three clues: loyal, honest, and protective.
   Khmer: ទាយមើល៖ តើឆ្នាំណា មានសំណាងជាងគេថ្ងៃនេះ? តម្រុយ៖ ស្មោះត្រង់ ត្រង់ និងការពារអ្នកដទៃ។

### Scene 8 – Goodbye – Chanda on camera (1:10–1:20)
Two-shot of Chanda and the dog together on the moonlit temple terrace, both clearly in frame. @Chanda, a beautiful Cambodian woman in her early twenties with a warm, kind and confident presence: oval face, soft defined cheekbones, warm amber-brown almond eyes, natural brows, rosy cheeks, natural skin with real texture and refined natural makeup, glossy black hair swept into a low side bun on her right side held by an elaborate golden lotus hairpin with fine dangling gold chains, delicate gold flower earrings, a fitted cream-and-gold Khmer silk brocade blouse with a high mandarin collar and long sleeves, a large round gold medallion on a chain engraved with all twelve zodiac animals, an ornate gold belt with a single ruby-red gemstone at the buckle, a deep navy silk sampot to the ankles with a vertical gold-embroidered front panel and a wide band of Khmer gold patterns at the hem, and gold bangles on both wrists, faces the camera, smiling and speaking to the viewer with natural lip movements. ## 180-second video – YouTube
A 180-second video about the Dog, today's luckiest animal: 18 clips of 10 seconds. Set the clip length in Flow to 10 seconds and add Chanda and the saved character @Dog as ingredients. Chanda opens the video on camera with the hook and narrates the rest off screen. The animals never speak or move their mouths: they only act. For each scene, press "Copy + English voice" or "Copy + Khmer voice": it copies the style line, the scene and the voice together. Put the clips in order 1 to 18.

### Topic and audience
A three-minute guide to the Dog's Great Day: meaning, character, love, work, money and lucky details. For people born in the Year of the Dog and zodiac fans on YouTube Shorts.

### Opening hook – 5 options
★ 1. Daily fortune
   English: Born in the Year of the Dog? Here's your lucky color, best time, and numbers for today, plus one gentle reminder.
   Khmer: អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំចមែនទេ? នេះគឺពណ៌ ពេលវេលា និងលេខសំណាងរបស់អ្នកថ្ងៃនេះ ព្រមទាំងការរំលឹកមួយផង។
2. Curiosity
   English: Out of all twelve zodiac signs, only one has a Great Day today. Is it yours?
   Khmer: ក្នុងចំណោមរាសីទាំង 12 មានតែមួយគត់ ដែលមានថ្ងៃល្អខ្លាំងនៅថ្ងៃនេះ។ តើជារាសីរបស់អ្នកឬទេ?
3. Personality
   English: If you were born in the Year of the Dog, people may describe you as loyal, honest, and protective. Today, that side of you shines.
   Khmer: បើអ្នកកើតឆ្នាំច មនុស្សជុំវិញខ្លួន ប្រហែលជាស្គាល់អ្នកថា ស្មោះត្រង់ ត្រង់ និងការពារអ្នកដទៃ។ ថ្ងៃនេះ ចំណុចល្អនោះកំពុងភ្លឺ។
4. Challenge
   English: Can you guess today's luckiest zodiac animal? Three clues: loyal, honest, and protective.
   Khmer: ទាយមើល៖ តើឆ្នាំណា មានសំណាងជាងគេថ្ងៃនេះ? តម្រុយ៖ ស្មោះត្រង់ ត្រង់ និងការពារអ្នកដទៃ។

## Voice-over – 180-second video
The same lines as the voice in each scene above, all together, for recording the voice yourself or with text-to-speech (then copy the scenes with plain "Copy", so Flow does not add a voice too).

### English
Scene 1 (0:00–0:10)
Born in the Year of the Dog? Here's your lucky color, best time, and numbers for today, plus one gentle reminder.

Scene 2 (0:10–0:20)
Today, Wednesday, 2 December 2026, is a Dog day. It is a Great Day for everyone born in the Year of the Dog. This is your day to shine!

Scene 3 (0:20–0:30)
Faithful guardian of the home, the Dog symbolizes loyalty, protection, and honest friendship. Today, this good energy is with you.

Scene 4 (0:30–0:40)
People born in the Year of the Dog are loyal, honest, and protective. Dogs are deeply trustworthy and stand up for what they believe in.

Scene 5 (0:40–0:50)
Born in the Year of the Dog? Your luck is strong today. Stay loyal, warm and protective. Say yes to good chances, and share your happiness with the people around you.

Scene 6 (0:50–1:00)
Love and family today: A good day to reach out, share a meal and show your affection openly.

Scene 7 (1:00–1:10)
Work today: Customers and coworkers respond well to you. A good day to present your ideas and close a deal.

Scene 8 (1:10–1:20)
Money today: Good for fair deals and collecting what is owed. Keep your records neat.

Scene 9 (1:20–1:30)
Your lucky color today is red. Wear something red, or carry a small red item with you, to keep good luck close.

Scene 10 (1:30–1:40)
Your best time today is from 11 in the morning to 1 in the afternoon. It is a good time for important plans, meetings, or a fresh start.

Scene 11 (1:40–1:50)
Your lucky direction today is the south. When you start something new, face the south, and walk forward with a calm and happy heart.

Scene 12 (1:50–2:00)
For lucky numbers: 3, 93, and 352. Comment your lucky number below, and let's say it again: 3, 93, 352!

Scene 13 (2:00–2:10)
The Dog's lucky days of the week are Monday and Thursday. Keep them in mind for plans that matter to you.

Scene 14 (2:10–2:20)
A gentle reminder: people born in the Year of the Dog can be anxious or overly suspicious of new situations. Today, stay balanced, and listen to your heart.

Scene 15 (2:20–2:30)
Make a small wish today, and take one kind step toward it. A peaceful heart helps good luck find its way to you.

Scene 16 (2:30–2:40)
Before the day ends, take a quiet moment to say thank you. Thank the people who help you, and thank yourself for trying your best.

Scene 17 (2:40–2:50)
Let's remember today's luck. Lucky color: red. Best time: from 11 in the morning to 1 in the afternoon. Lucky direction: south. Lucky numbers: 3, 93, and 352.

Scene 18 (2:50–3:00)
That's your Chinese zodiac fortune for today. See all twelve signs and your lucky numbers at mybirthsign dot com. Follow us for tomorrow's fortune. Have a lucky day!

5

6

7
00:00:17,881 --> 00:00:19,950

8

9
00:00:22,426 --> 00:00:24,509

11

12

13

14

15

និងតែងតែការពារអ្វីដែលខ្លួនជឿជាក់។

16

17

18
00:00:44,135 --> 00:00:46,792

19
00:00:46,842 --> 00:00:49,950
ហើយចែករំលែកភាពរីករាយ ដល់មនុស្សជុំវិញខ្លួន។

20

21
00:00:52,500 --> 00:00:56,950
ជាថ្ងៃល្អសម្រាប់ទាក់ទងគ្នា ញ៉ាំអាហារជាមួយគ្នា

22

23
00:01:00,000 --> 00:01:01,987

24
00:01:02,037 --> 00:01:05,506
និងមិត្តរួមការងារ ឆ្លើយតបល្អចំពោះអ្នក។

25

26

30

31

32

33

ជាប់ខ្លួន ដើម្បីរក្សាសំណាងល្អ ឲ្យនៅជិតអ្នក។

34

35

36

38

39

43

52

53

54

ហើយធ្វើរឿងល្អមួយ ដើម្បីឈានទៅរកក្ដីប្រាថ្នានោះ។

55

58

62

63

64

65
00:02:50,000 --> 00:02:52,258

66
00:02:52,308 --> 00:02:54,373

00:02:54,423 --> 00:02:56,232

68

## Long YouTube video – all 12 signs (about 5 minutes)
A long YouTube video (5:10): today's luckiest animal, an overview, then all 12 signs in order (two 10-second clips each), then a short closing. Lucky numbers are given only for today's lucky signs. Add Chanda and the 12 saved characters as ingredients (@Rat, @Ox … @Pig; each sign's clips name its own character). Chanda opens the video on camera and narrates the rest off screen; the animals never speak. Set the clip length in Flow to 10 seconds. For each scene, press "Copy + English voice" or "Copy + Khmer voice". Put the clips in order 1 to 31.

### YouTube chapters (paste into the description)
0:00 Introduction
0:40 Rat (ជូត) – Ordinary Day
1:00 Ox (ឆ្លូវ) – Ordinary Day

2:40 Horse (មមី) – Good Day
3:00 Goat (មមែ) – Ordinary Day

3:40 Rooster (រកា) – Ordinary Day
4:00 Dog (ច) – Great Day
4:20 Pig (កុរ) – Ordinary Day
4:40 Closing

### Topic and audience
A full daily fortune for all twelve Chinese zodiac signs. For anyone who wants their own sign's rating, lucky color, best time and direction; chapters let each viewer jump to their sign.

### Thumbnail prompt – YouTube (16:9)
Make the picture in an image AI (for example Gemini or Flow) with Chanda's picture and the saved zodiac characters (named after their animals) added as references. Upload size: 16:9, at least 640 pixels wide (3840 x 2160 is best), JPG or PNG, under 2 MB if you upload from your phone.

With the words drawn in (English):
Eye-catching YouTube thumbnail, horizontal 16:9, 3840 x 2160. Photorealistic still from a high-budget fantasy epic blending Khmer royal heritage with a Chinese historical fantasy drama: real human faces with natural skin, lifelike zodiac animals in engraved ceremonial gold armor with real metallic reflections, very expressive faces and eyes. Background: a glowing Khmer temple inspired by Angkor Wat under an enormous full moon and a deep indigo starry sky, lotus pool, golden particles. Bright, high-contrast, rich colors (imperial gold, deep navy, ruby red) that stand out on a small phone screen; strong golden rim light separating the characters from the background; slightly blurred background so the faces pop. Big, simple composition with only two or three subjects. Keep the bottom-right corner free (YouTube puts the video length there). No watermark, no logo, no YouTube buttons or arrows, no cartoon or plastic look. The twelve golden-armored zodiac guardians in a tight arc across the frame, all looking at the camera with excited faces; three of them shine much brighter with golden auras, one rests calmly in a soft blue bubble of light. Large bold text, at most a few words, in thick rounded sans-serif capitals with a dark outline and a soft drop shadow, perfectly spelled: "ALL 12 SIGNS" and "TODAY". The text sits across the top of the frame in the dark sky, never over a face.

### Audio design
Music: Gentle magical background music with soft chimes and a light Asian flute. No other voices: the animals do not speak. Keep it well below the voice (about 20 dB quieter) so every word is clear. Sound effects, used lightly: a soft chime when a lucky fact is revealed, a gentle whoosh on scene changes, a warm swell at the ending. No loud or scary sounds.

## Voice-over – story video
The same lines as the voice in each scene above, all together, for recording the voice yourself or with text-to-speech (then copy the scenes with plain "Copy", so Flow does not add a voice too).

### English
Scene 1 (0:00–0:10)
Can one little golden dog wake the night flowers before the full moon rises? Once upon a time, beside an ancient temple in Cambodia, our story begins.

Scene 2 (0:10–0:20)
But this morning, the flowers fell into a deep sleep. Nothing could wake them. Without their glow, the festival could not begin.

Scene 3 (0:20–0:30)
In the middle of the pond, a golden lotus opened, and out came a little golden dog. Today was a Dog day, and the dog felt something special.

Scene 4 (0:30–0:40)
A little frog named Kong came hopping over. "Little Dog," he said, "only you can wake the night flowers. Today is your lucky day!"

Scene 5 (0:40–0:50)
Faithful guardian of the home, the Dog symbolizes loyalty, protection, and honest friendship. Kong smiled. That was exactly why the moon had chosen it.

Scene 6 (0:50–1:00)
To wake the night flowers, the little hero needed three lucky treasures: its lucky color, its lucky path, and its lucky numbers.

Scene 7 (1:00–1:10)
"Let's go!" said the dog, and it set off on its adventure, with Kong right by its side.

Scene 8 (1:10–1:20)
People born in the Year of the Dog are loyal, honest, and protective. Dogs are deeply trustworthy and stand up for what they believe in.

Scene 9 (1:20–1:30)
The first treasure was waiting in a garden of a thousand lanterns. Only one shone brightest. It was red, the dog's lucky color today.

Scene 10 (1:30–1:40)
The dog tied a red ribbon around its neck. "Wear your lucky color," said Kong, "and good luck stays close to you."

Scene 11 (1:40–1:50)
Next, they came to a crossroads with many paths. One path began to glow. It pointed to the south, the dog's lucky direction.

Scene 12 (1:50–2:00)
But the path led into a dark, quiet forest. The dog stopped. Sometimes, people born in the Year of the Dog can be anxious or overly suspicious of new situations.

Scene 13 (2:00–2:10)
Kong came close and smiled. "Breathe slowly," he said. "Trust your heart. You are stronger than you think."

Scene 14 (2:10–2:20)
Step by step, the dog walked into the forest. Its golden glow lit up the trees, and soon the whole forest was full of light.

Scene 15 (2:20–2:30)
The little hero learned that its worries were small, and its good heart was big. Balance and courage always help us grow.

Scene 16 (2:30–2:40)
At last, they reached a great stone door in the temple. Carved above it were bells, stairs, and a riddle about lucky numbers.

Scene 17 (2:40–2:50)
"The door opens only at your best time," said Kong. For the Dog today, that is from 11 in the morning to 1 in the afternoon. They waited patiently.

Scene 18 (2:50–3:00)
When the time came, the dog rang the golden bells 3 times, its first lucky number. Ding! The door began to shine.

Scene 19 (3:00–3:10)
The door opened, and a stairway of light appeared. The dog climbed 93 glowing steps, its second lucky number, up to the top of the tower.

Scene 20 (3:10–3:20)
At the top of the tower, they found a single drop of golden dew, sparkling in a crystal shell. It was the magic that wakes the flowers!

Scene 21 (3:20–3:30)
Around the dew drop, seven little lights twinkled, one for each day of the week. Monday and Thursday shone brightest, the Dog's lucky days.

Scene 22 (3:30–3:40)
Time was running out. Holding the dew drop carefully, the little hero hurried home, with Kong right beside it.

Scene 23 (3:40–3:50)
Back at the pond, everyone was waiting. The night was dark, and the full moon was rising. Was it too late?

Scene 24 (3:50–4:00)
The dog let the golden dew fall on the flowers. For a moment, nothing happened. Then, one by one, the flowers opened and glowed!

Scene 25 (4:00–4:10)
The night flowers glowed again. Lanterns rose into the sky, and the full-moon festival began. Everyone cheered for the little golden hero!

Scene 26 (4:10–4:20)
The full moon shone down warmly, as if to say thank you. The dog felt peaceful and proud. It had done its very best.

Scene 27 (4:20–4:30)
If you were born in the Year of the Dog, today is your day too. Like our hero, be brave, be kind, and follow your good heart.

Scene 28 (4:30–4:40)
Let's remember today's luck. Lucky color: red. Best time: from 11 in the morning to 1 in the afternoon. Lucky direction: south. Lucky numbers: 3, 93, and 352.

Scene 29 (4:40–4:50)
And so, under the bright full moon, the little golden hero and Kong fell fast asleep, dreaming of tomorrow.

Scene 30 (4:50–5:00)
That's your Chinese zodiac fortune for today. See all twelve signs and your lucky numbers at mybirthsign dot com. Follow us for tomorrow's fortune. Have a lucky day!

00:00:14,146 --> 00:00:16,048
គ្មានអ្វីអាចដាស់វាបានទេ។

8
00:00:16,098 --> 00:00:17,511
បើគ្មានពន្លឺរបស់វា

9
00:00:17,561 --> 00:00:19,950
ពិធីបុណ្យមិនអាចចាប់ផ្ដើមបានទេ។

10
00:00:20,000 --> 00:00:22,998
នៅកណ្ដាលស្រះ ផ្កាឈូកមាសមួយបានរីក

11
00:00:23,048 --> 00:00:25,474
ហើយមានឆ្កែមាសតូចមួយ ចេញមក។

12
00:00:25,524 --> 00:00:27,283
ថ្ងៃនេះជាថ្ងៃឆ្នាំច

13
00:00:27,333 --> 00:00:29,950
ហើយឆ្កែមានអារម្មណ៍ពិសេសណាស់។

14
00:00:30,000 --> 00:00:32,586
កង្កែបតូចមួយ ឈ្មោះគង់ លោតតម្រង់មក។

15
00:00:32,636 --> 00:00:34,291
គង់និយាយថា "ឆ្កែតូចអើយ

16
00:00:34,341 --> 00:00:36,849
មានតែអ្នកទេ ដែលអាចដាស់ផ្កាពេលយប់។

17
00:00:36,899 --> 00:00:39,020
ថ្ងៃនេះ ជាថ្ងៃសំណាងរបស់អ្នក!

18
00:00:39,070 --> 00:00:39,950
"

19
00:00:40,000 --> 00:00:42,210
ឆ្កែ គឺជាអ្នកយាមផ្ទះដ៏ស្មោះត្រង់។

20
00:00:42,260 --> 00:00:44,197
ឆ្នាំចតំណាងឲ្យភាពស្មោះស្ម័គ្រ

21
00:00:44,247 --> 00:00:46,457
ការការពារ និងមិត្តភាពដ៏ពិតប្រាកដ។

22
00:00:46,507 --> 00:00:47,279
គង់ញញឹម។

23
00:00:47,329 --> 00:00:49,950
នេះហើយ ជាហេតុដែលព្រះចន្ទ បានជ្រើសរើសវា។

24
00:00:50,000 --> 00:00:53,394
ដើម្បីដាស់ផ្កាពេលយប់ វីរបុរសតូច

25
00:00:53,444 --> 00:00:56,394
ត្រូវការរតនសម្បត្តិសំណាងបី៖

26
00:00:56,444 --> 00:00:59,950
ពណ៌សំណាង ផ្លូវសំណាង និងលេខសំណាង។

27
00:01:00,000 --> 00:01:02,483
ឆ្កែនិយាយថា "តោះទៅ!

28
00:01:02,533 --> 00:01:05,950
" ហើយវាក៏ចេញដំណើរផ្សងព្រេង

29
00:01:06,000 --> 00:01:09,950
ដោយមានគង់ នៅក្បែរខ្លួនជានិច្ច។

30
00:01:10,000 --> 00:01:13,027
អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំច ជាមនុស្សស្មោះត្រង់

31
00:01:13,077 --> 00:01:15,238
ត្រង់ និងការពារអ្នកដទៃ។

32
00:01:15,288 --> 00:01:16,777
ចជាទីទុកចិត្តបាន

33
00:01:16,827 --> 00:01:19,950
និងតែងតែការពារអ្វីដែលខ្លួនជឿជាក់។

34
00:01:20,000 --> 00:01:22,208
រតនសម្បត្តិទីមួយ កំពុងរង់ចាំ

35
00:01:22,258 --> 00:01:24,144
នៅក្នុងសួនចង្កៀងមួយពាន់។

36
00:01:24,194 --> 00:01:26,563
មានតែមួយប៉ុណ្ណោះ ដែលភ្លឺជាងគេ។

37
00:01:26,613 --> 00:01:29,950
វាគឺពណ៌ក្រហម ដែលជាពណ៌សំណាងរបស់ឆ្កែថ្ងៃនេះ។

38
00:01:30,000 --> 00:01:32,766
ឆ្កែចងខ្សែបូពណ៌ក្រហម ជុំវិញក។

39
00:01:32,816 --> 00:01:36,358
គង់និយាយថា "ស្លៀកពាក់ពណ៌សំណាងរបស់អ្នក

40
00:01:36,408 --> 00:01:38,785
ហើយសំណាងល្អ នឹងនៅជិតអ្នក។

41
00:01:38,835 --> 00:01:39,950
"

42
00:01:40,000 --> 00:01:42,723
បន្ទាប់មក ពួកគេមកដល់ផ្លូវបំបែកមួយ

43
00:01:42,773 --> 00:01:44,320
ដែលមានផ្លូវជាច្រើន។

44
00:01:44,370 --> 00:01:46,253
ផ្លូវមួយ ចាប់ផ្ដើមភ្លឺ។

45
00:01:46,303 --> 00:01:49,950
វាចង្អុលទៅទិសខាងត្បូង ដែលជាទិសសំណាងរបស់ឆ្កែ។

46
00:01:50,000 --> 00:01:53,008
ប៉ុន្តែផ្លូវនោះ នាំចូលទៅក្នុងព្រៃងងឹត

47
00:01:53,058 --> 00:01:54,495
និងស្ងាត់ស្ងៀមមួយ។

48
00:01:54,545 --> 00:01:55,487
ឆ្កែឈប់។

49
00:01:55,537 --> 00:01:58,049
អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំច ជួនកាលព្រួយបារម្ភ

50
00:01:58,099 --> 00:01:59,950
ឬសង្ស័យស្ថានភាពថ្មីពេក។

51
00:02:00,000 --> 00:02:01,650
គង់មកជិត ហើយញញឹម។

52
00:02:01,700 --> 00:02:04,150
គង់និយាយថា "ដកដង្ហើមយឺតៗ។

53
00:02:04,200 --> 00:02:05,950
ជឿលើចិត្តរបស់អ្នក។

54
00:02:06,000 --> 00:02:08,750
អ្នកខ្លាំងជាងអ្វីដែលអ្នកគិត។

55
00:02:08,800 --> 00:02:09,950
"

56
00:02:10,000 --> 00:02:13,128
ម្ដងមួយជំហានៗ ឆ្កែដើរចូលក្នុងព្រៃ។

57
00:02:13,178 --> 00:02:17,240
ពន្លឺមាសរបស់វា បំភ្លឺដើមឈើ ហើយមិនយូរប៉ុន្មាន

58
00:02:17,290 --> 00:02:19,950
ព្រៃទាំងមូល ក៏ពោរពេញដោយពន្លឺ។

59
00:02:20,000 --> 00:02:23,371
វីរបុរសតូចបានរៀនថា ការព្រួយបារម្ភរបស់វា

60
00:02:23,421 --> 00:02:26,003
តូចទេ តែចិត្តល្អរបស់វា ធំណាស់។

61
00:02:26,053 --> 00:02:29,950
តុល្យភាព និងភាពក្លាហាន តែងជួយឲ្យយើងរីកចម្រើន។

62
00:02:30,000 --> 00:02:32,950
ទីបំផុត ពួកគេមកដល់ទ្វារថ្មដ៏ធំមួយ

63
00:02:33,000 --> 00:02:34,314
នៅក្នុងប្រាសាទ។

64
00:02:34,364 --> 00:02:37,223
នៅខាងលើទ្វារ មានរូបចម្លាក់កណ្ដឹង

65
00:02:37,273 --> 00:02:39,950
ជណ្ដើរ និងប្រស្នាអំពីលេខសំណាង។

66
00:02:40,000 --> 00:02:41,500
គង់និយាយថា "ទ្វារនេះ

67
00:02:41,550 --> 00:02:44,214
បើកតែនៅពេលល្អបំផុតរបស់អ្នកប៉ុណ្ណោះ។

68
00:02:44,264 --> 00:02:45,764
" សម្រាប់ឆ្កែថ្ងៃនេះ

69
00:02:45,814 --> 00:02:48,167
គឺម៉ោង 11 ព្រឹក ដល់ម៉ោង 1 រសៀល។

70
00:02:48,217 --> 00:02:49,950
ពួកគេរង់ចាំដោយអត់ធ្មត់។

71
00:02:50,000 --> 00:02:52,822
ពេលដល់ម៉ោង ឆ្កែវាយកណ្ដឹងមាស

72
00:02:52,872 --> 00:02:56,652
ចំនួន 3 ដង ដែលជាលេខសំណាងទីមួយរបស់វា។

73
00:02:56,702 --> 00:02:57,929
ទីង!

74
00:02:57,979 --> 00:02:59,950
ទ្វារចាប់ផ្ដើមភ្លឺ។

75
00:03:00,000 --> 00:03:03,254
ទ្វារបើក ហើយមានជណ្ដើរពន្លឺមួយ លេចឡើង។

76
00:03:03,304 --> 00:03:05,932
ឆ្កែឡើងជណ្ដើរភ្លឺ ចំនួន 93 កាំ

77
00:03:05,982 --> 00:03:09,950
ដែលជាលេខសំណាងទីពីររបស់វា រហូតដល់កំពូលប្រាង្គ។

78
00:03:10,000 --> 00:03:11,405
នៅលើកំពូលប្រាង្គ

79
00:03:11,455 --> 00:03:14,677
ពួកគេបានរកឃើញដំណក់ទឹកសន្សើមពណ៌មាសមួយ

80
00:03:14,727 --> 00:03:17,586
ភ្លឺចែងចាំង ក្នុងសំបកគ្រីស្តាល់។

81
00:03:17,636 --> 00:03:19,950
វាគឺជាវេទមន្ត ដែលដាស់ផ្កា!

82
00:03:20,000 --> 00:03:21,388
នៅជុំវិញដំណក់ទឹកសន្សើម

83
00:03:21,438 --> 00:03:23,741
មានពន្លឺតូចៗប្រាំពីរ ភ្លឺភ្លឹបភ្លែតៗ

84
00:03:23,791 --> 00:03:25,898
មួយសម្រាប់ថ្ងៃនីមួយៗក្នុងសប្ដាហ៍។

85
00:03:25,948 --> 00:03:27,662
ថ្ងៃច័ន្ទ និងថ្ងៃព្រហស្បតិ៍

86
00:03:27,712 --> 00:03:29,950
ភ្លឺជាងគេ ដែលជាថ្ងៃសំណាងរបស់ឆ្នាំច។

87
00:03:30,000 --> 00:03:31,539
ពេលវេលាជិតអស់ហើយ។

88
00:03:31,589 --> 00:03:35,838
វីរបុរសតូច កាន់ដំណក់ទឹកសន្សើមយ៉ាងប្រុងប្រយ័ត្ន

89
00:03:35,888 --> 00:03:39,950
ហើយប្រញាប់ត្រឡប់ទៅផ្ទះវិញ ដោយមានគង់ នៅក្បែរ។

90
00:03:40,000 --> 00:03:44,485
នៅឯស្រះវិញ អ្នកទាំងអស់គ្នា កំពុងរង់ចាំ។

91
00:03:44,535 --> 00:03:48,438
យប់ងងឹត ហើយព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់ កំពុងរះ។

92
00:03:48,488 --> 00:03:49,950
តើយឺតពេលហើយឬ?

93
00:03:50,000 --> 00:03:53,093
ឆ្កែទម្លាក់ទឹកសន្សើមមាស ទៅលើផ្កា។

94
00:03:53,143 --> 00:03:55,760
មួយសន្ទុះ គ្មានអ្វីកើតឡើងទេ។

95
00:03:55,810 --> 00:03:59,950
បន្ទាប់មក ផ្កាម្ដងមួយៗ ក៏រីក ហើយភ្លឺចែងចាំង!

96
00:04:00,000 --> 00:04:01,897
ផ្កាពេលយប់ ភ្លឺឡើងវិញ។

97
00:04:01,947 --> 00:04:03,136
គោមហោះឡើងលើមេឃ

98
00:04:03,186 --> 00:04:06,587
ហើយពិធីបុណ្យព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់ ក៏ចាប់ផ្ដើម។

99
00:04:06,637 --> 00:04:09,950
អ្នកទាំងអស់គ្នា អបអរសាទរវីរបុរសមាសតូច!

100
00:04:10,000 --> 00:04:13,121
ព្រះចន្ទពេញវង់ បញ្ចាំងពន្លឺកក់ក្ដៅចុះមក

101
00:04:13,171 --> 00:04:14,991
ហាក់ដូចជាពោលពាក្យអរគុណ។

102
00:04:15,041 --> 00:04:17,836
ឆ្កែមានអារម្មណ៍ស្ងប់ និងមានមោទនភាព។

103
00:04:17,886 --> 00:04:19,950
វាបានព្យាយាមអស់ពីសមត្ថភាព។

104
00:04:20,000 --> 00:04:23,810
បើអ្នកកើតឆ្នាំច ថ្ងៃនេះ ក៏ជាថ្ងៃរបស់អ្នកដែរ។

105
00:04:23,860 --> 00:04:26,529
ដូចវីរបុរសតូចរបស់យើង សូមក្លាហាន

106
00:04:26,579 --> 00:04:29,950
មានចិត្តល្អ ហើយធ្វើតាមចិត្តល្អរបស់អ្នក។

107
00:04:30,000 --> 00:04:31,529
សូមចងចាំសំណាងថ្ងៃនេះ។

108
00:04:31,579 --> 00:04:32,958
ពណ៌សំណាង៖ ពណ៌ក្រហម។

109
00:04:33,008 --> 00:04:35,739
ពេលល្អ៖ ម៉ោង 11 ព្រឹក ដល់ម៉ោង 1 រសៀល។

110
00:04:35,789 --> 00:04:37,394
ទិសសំណាង៖ ទិសខាងត្បូង។

111
00:04:37,444 --> 00:04:39,950
លេខសំណាង៖ លេខ 3 លេខ 93 និងលេខ 352។

112
00:04:40,000 --> 00:04:44,155

```
1
00:00:00,000 --> 00:00:02,671
Can one little golden dog wake the night

3
00:00:05,102 --> 00:00:07,909
Once upon a time, beside an ancient temple

4
00:00:07,959 --> 00:00:09,950

5
00:00:10,000 --> 00:00:11,261
But this morning

6
00:00:11,311 --> 00:00:14,130

7
00:00:14,180 --> 00:00:16,098

8
00:00:16,148 --> 00:00:17,573

9
00:00:17,623 --> 00:00:19,950

10
00:00:20,000 --> 00:00:22,965
In the middle of the pond, a golden lotus

11

12
00:00:26,029 --> 00:00:27,376
Today was a Dog day

13
00:00:27,426 --> 00:00:29,950

14
00:00:30,000 --> 00:00:31,470
A little frog named

00:00:31,520 --> 00:00:33,310

16
00:00:33,360 --> 00:00:34,990

17
00:00:35,040 --> 00:00:37,950

18
00:00:38,000 --> 00:00:39,950

19
00:00:40,000 --> 00:00:41,964
Faithful guardian of the home

20
00:00:42,014 --> 00:00:43,769

21
00:00:43,819 --> 00:00:46,131

22
00:00:46,181 --> 00:00:46,964
Kong smiled.

23
00:00:47,014 --> 00:00:48,353

24
00:00:48,403 --> 00:00:49,950

25
00:00:50,000 --> 00:00:51,983
To wake the night flowers

26
00:00:52,033 --> 00:00:54,259

27
00:00:54,309 --> 00:00:56,861

28
00:00:56,911 --> 00:00:59,950

29
00:01:00,000 --> 00:01:02,842

31
00:01:06,627 --> 00:01:09,950

32
00:01:10,000 --> 00:01:12,506

33
00:01:12,556 --> 00:01:15,063

34
00:01:15,113 --> 00:01:17,093

35
00:01:17,143 --> 00:01:19,950

36

37
00:01:22,308 --> 00:01:24,950

38
00:01:25,000 --> 00:01:26,873

39
00:01:26,923 --> 00:01:29,950

40
00:01:30,000 --> 00:01:33,734

41
00:01:33,784 --> 00:01:36,797

42

43

44
00:01:42,605 --> 00:01:43,900

46
00:01:45,882 --> 00:01:47,765

47
00:01:47,815 --> 00:01:49,950

48
00:01:50,000 --> 00:01:51,296
But the path led into

49
00:01:51,346 --> 00:01:52,642

50
00:01:52,692 --> 00:01:53,668
The dog stopped.

51
00:01:53,718 --> 00:01:55,078

52
00:01:55,128 --> 00:01:57,450

53
00:01:57,500 --> 00:01:59,950

54

55

56

60

63

64

65

67

68

69
00:02:40,000 --> 00:02:41,368
"The door opens only

70

71

For the Dog today

72

73

74

75

76

77

78

79

80

81
00:03:01,154 --> 00:03:03,642

82
00:03:03,692 --> 00:03:06,104

83

84
00:03:07,923 --> 00:03:09,950

85

86

87

88
00:03:16,923 --> 00:03:19,950

89

91

92

93

94
00:03:32,000 --> 00:03:34,807

95
00:03:34,857 --> 00:03:37,474

96
00:03:37,524 --> 00:03:39,950

97
00:03:40,000 --> 00:03:43,774

98

99

100
00:03:48,431 --> 00:03:49,950

101
00:03:50,000 --> 00:03:51,739
The dog let the golden

102

103

For a moment, nothing happened.

104
00:03:56,260 --> 00:03:57,511

106

107
00:04:02,331 --> 00:04:04,236
Lanterns rose into the sky

108

109
00:04:06,767 --> 00:04:07,920

## Take-it-easy video – the unlucky sign, Dragon (8 scenes, 80 seconds)
An 80-second video for the Dragon, today's sign to take it easy (in the traditional day cycle the Dragon clashes with the Dog day): 8 clips of 10 seconds. Set the clip length in Flow to 10 seconds and add Chanda and the saved character @Dragon as ingredients (the Dragon is the only zodiac character in this video, from the first scene to the last). Chanda is on camera and speaks with lip-sync in scenes 1 and 8; in the other scenes she narrates off screen. The animals never speak or move their mouths: they only act. It is one continuous little story in one place (the dragon hurries, wobbles, stops to breathe, a little lotus lantern keeps it company, it climbs the temple steps and looks out with hope), so make the clips in order and keep the same setting and light. No lucky numbers or colors in this video: it is about encouragement. Keep it gentle and realistic: a quiet day, not bad luck. For each scene, press "Copy + English voice" or "Copy + Khmer voice". Put the clips in order 1 to 8.

### Scene 1 – The hook – Chanda on camera (0:00–0:10)

She starts by raising one open hand toward the camera like a friendly "stop" sign, with a caring, slightly urgent expression, then lowers it and keeps talking warmly. Below her, at the edge of the pond where a path of flat stepping stones leads up to the temple, @Dragon (the Dragon guardian with their dragon) sits looking a little tired and lets out a quiet sigh with its mouth closed; it does not speak. Medium shot, gentle push-in toward Chanda. Chanda is the saved Flow character @Chanda; the dragon is the saved Flow character @Dragon. ### Scene 2 – A wobbly start (0:10–0:20)

## Voice-over – take-it-easy video
The same lines as the voice in each scene above, all together, for recording the voice yourself or with text-to-speech (then copy the scenes with plain "Copy", so Flow does not add a voice too).

### English
Scene 1 (0:00–0:10)
Stop scrolling! Born in the Year of the Dragon? Your luck isn't good today. But don't worry, watch to the end.

Scene 2 (0:10–0:20)
Today, 2 December, is a Dog day, opposite the Dragon in the zodiac circle. Some days don't go to plan.

Scene 3 (0:20–0:30)
And that's okay. Everyone has days like this. A quiet day is not a bad day. It is a day to breathe.

Scene 4 (0:30–0:40)
Be careful today: you can come across as proud or impatient with limitations. Pause before you react. Take a deep breath first.

Scene 5 (0:40–0:50)
Be gentle with the people you love, and with yourself. And lean on a friend you trust.

Scene 6 (0:50–1:00)
At work and with money, take small, careful steps. Finish one thing, then the next. Small steps still count.

Scene 7 (1:00–1:10)
Slow is still moving forward. Every quiet day makes you stronger, and brighter days are already on their way.

Scene 8 (1:10–1:20)
Rest well tonight. Tomorrow is a new day, and it could be yours. Follow MyBirthSign for your fortune every day!

## Long YouTube video – full fortune, 8:40 (mid-roll ads)
A full daily fortune for YouTube (8:40, 52 clips of 10 seconds), long enough for mid-roll ads. In order: the hook, the Great Day sign (Dog) in depth, the two Good Day signs (Tiger and Horse), the sign to take it easy (Dragon) with its little story, the other eight signs, then a recap and goodbye. Add Chanda and the 12 saved characters as ingredients in Flow (@Rat, @Ox … @Pig; each part names its own character). Chanda is on camera and speaks with lip-sync in scenes 1, 4 and 52; in the others she narrates off screen. The animals never speak or move their mouths: they only act. Set the clip length to 10 seconds, make the clips in order 1 to 52, and paste the chapters into the YouTube description.

### YouTube chapters (paste into the description)
0:00 Introduction
0:40 Great Day: Dog
2:40 Good Day: Tiger
3:30 Good Day: Horse
4:20 Take it easy: Dragon
5:20 All the other signs
8:00 Recap and goodbye

### Opening hook – 5 options
1. Every sign in one line
   English: One sign has a Great Day today, two have a Good Day, and one should slow down. Which one are you?
   Khmer: ថ្ងៃនេះ មានមួយឆ្នាំថ្ងៃល្អខ្លាំង ពីរឆ្នាំថ្ងៃល្អ និងមួយឆ្នាំគួរធ្វើអ្វីៗយឺតៗ។ តើអ្នកជាឆ្នាំណា?
2. Before a big decision
   English: Before you make any big decision today, watch this: your lucky color, your best time, and whether to slow down.
   Khmer: មុននឹងសម្រេចចិត្តរឿងធំថ្ងៃនេះ សូមមើលវីដេអូនេះ៖ ពណ៌សំណាង ពេលល្អ និងថាតើគួរធ្វើយឺតៗឬទេ។
★ 3. Speak to the careful sign
   English: Born in the Year of the Dragon? Watch this before you do anything big today. Everyone else, your luck is here too.
   Khmer: អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំរោងមែនទេ? សូមមើលវីដេអូនេះ មុនធ្វើរឿងធំថ្ងៃនេះ។ អ្នកផ្សេងទៀត សំណាងរបស់អ្នកក៏នៅទីនេះដែរ។
4. Name the winner
   English: Today the Dog has its Great Day, and one sign should take it easy. Is it yours?
   Khmer: ថ្ងៃនេះ ឆ្នាំចមានថ្ងៃល្អខ្លាំង ហើយមានមួយឆ្នាំគួរប្រុងប្រយ័ត្ន។ តើជាឆ្នាំរបស់អ្នកឬទេ?
5. Comment first
   English: Comment your sign now. By the end of this video, you'll know your lucky color, best time, and direction for today.
   Khmer: សូមសរសេររាសីរបស់អ្នកឥឡូវនេះ។ មុនវីដេអូនេះចប់ អ្នកនឹងដឹងពណ៌ ពេល និងទិសសំណាងថ្ងៃនេះ។
★ = used to open this video today (the hook changes from day to day, and each one gives every viewer a reason to stay to find their own sign). To use another hook, replace the voice line in scene 1.

### Mid-roll ads
YouTube lets you add ad breaks in the middle of videos that are 8 minutes or longer; this one is 8:40. Put the breaks at chapter starts so they don't cut a sentence. Chapter starts after 2:00: 2:40, 3:30, 4:20, 5:20, 8:00. Two or three breaks are enough; too many make viewers leave.

## Voice-over – full fortune video
The same lines as the voice in each scene above, all together, for recording the voice yourself or with text-to-speech (then copy the scenes with plain "Copy", so Flow does not add a voice too).

ថ្ងៃសំណាងរបស់ឆ្នាំច គឺថ្ងៃច័ន្ទ និងថ្ងៃព្រហស្បតិ៍។ សូមទុកសម្រាប់ផែនការសំខាន់ៗ។ សូមចែករំលែកសំណាង។

ថ្ងៃនេះ សូមប្រាថ្នាអ្វីមួយតូចៗ ហើយធ្វើរឿងល្អមួយ ដើម្បីឈានទៅរកក្ដីប្រាថ្នានោះ។ ចិត្តដែលស្ងប់ នឹងជួយនាំសំណាងល្អ មករកអ្នក។

Scene 51 (8:20–8:30)
មុនថ្ងៃនេះបញ្ចប់ សូមអរគុណ៖ អរគុណមនុស្សដែលជួយអ្នក និងអរគុណខ្លួនឯង។ សូមចែករំលែកសំណាង។

Scene 52 (8:30–8:40)
សូម subscribe និងតាមដាន MyBirthSign រៀងរាល់ថ្ងៃ។ តើអ្នកកើតឆ្នាំអ្វី? ប្រាប់ខ្ញុំក្នុងមតិយោបល់ផង! ថ្ងៃធាតុលោហៈ!

សូមប្រយ័ត្នថ្ងៃនេះ៖ ជួនកាលមើលទៅអំនួត

103
00:04:43,186 --> 00:04:45,437

104

105

សូមដកដង្ហើមវែងៗជាមុនសិន។

106

សូមទន់ភ្លន់ចំពោះមនុស្សដែលអ្នកស្រឡាញ់

107
00:04:54,444 --> 00:04:56,370
និងចំពោះខ្លួនឯង។

108

109

110
00:05:01,947 --> 00:05:04,817
សូមបោះជំហានតូចៗ ដោយប្រុងប្រយ័ត្ន។

111
00:05:04,867 --> 00:05:07,826

112
00:05:07,876 --> 00:05:09,950

113

116

117

### Subtitles – English (2026-12-02-yt8.en.srt)
```
1
00:00:00,000 --> 00:00:02,743
Born in the Year of the Dragon?

2
00:00:02,793 --> 00:00:04,635
Watch this before you

3
00:00:04,685 --> 00:00:06,617
do anything big today.

4
00:00:06,667 --> 00:00:09,950
Everyone else, your luck is here too.

5
00:00:10,000 --> 00:00:11,795
Today is a Dog day.

6
00:00:11,845 --> 00:00:14,610
Coming up: one Great Day sign

7
00:00:14,660 --> 00:00:16,843
two Good Days, one sign

8
00:00:16,893 --> 00:00:19,950
to take it easy, and all twelve.

9
00:00:20,000 --> 00:00:23,379
Dog, Tiger and Horse are lucky today

10
00:00:23,429 --> 00:00:26,426
and the Dragon should slow down.

11
00:00:26,476 --> 00:00:29,950
Find your sign in the chapters below.

12
00:00:30,000 --> 00:00:31,392
Before we start

13
00:00:31,442 --> 00:00:35,046
comment your sign below, and subscribe

14
00:00:35,096 --> 00:00:38,315
so you get your fortune every day.

15
00:00:38,365 --> 00:00:39,950
A Yang Metal day!

16
00:00:40,000 --> 00:00:42,881
Wednesday, 2 December: a Great Day

17
00:00:42,931 --> 00:00:46,416
for everyone born in the Year of the Dog!

18
00:00:46,466 --> 00:00:49,950
Let's see what this lucky day brings you.

19
00:00:50,000 --> 00:00:52,686
Faithful guardian of the home

20
00:00:52,736 --> 00:00:55,139
the Dog symbolizes loyalty

21
00:00:55,189 --> 00:00:58,346
protection, and honest friendship.

22
00:00:58,396 --> 00:00:59,950
Let it guide you.

23
00:01:00,000 --> 00:01:02,205
People born in the Year

24
00:01:02,255 --> 00:01:05,146
of the Dog are often described

25
00:01:05,196 --> 00:01:08,381
as loyal, honest, and protective.

26
00:01:08,431 --> 00:01:09,950
Share your luck.

27
00:01:10,000 --> 00:01:12,881
This is your day.

28
00:01:12,931 --> 00:01:19,950
Small good choices add up to a lucky day.

29
00:01:20,000 --> 00:01:21,987
Love and family today:

30
00:01:22,037 --> 00:01:25,413
A good day to reach out, share a meal

31
00:01:25,463 --> 00:01:28,283
and show your affection openly.

32
00:01:28,333 --> 00:01:29,950
Keep a calm heart.

33
00:01:30,000 --> 00:01:31,969
Work today: Customers

34
00:01:32,019 --> 00:01:35,238
and coworkers respond well to you.

35
00:01:35,288 --> 00:01:38,315
A good day to present your ideas

36
00:01:38,365 --> 00:01:39,950
and close a deal.

37
00:01:40,000 --> 00:01:43,182
Money today: Good for fair deals

38
00:01:43,232 --> 00:01:46,011
and collecting what is owed.

39
00:01:46,061 --> 00:01:48,334
Keep your records neat.

40
00:01:48,384 --> 00:01:49,950
Share your luck.

41
00:01:50,000 --> 00:01:52,298
Today's lucky color is red.

42
00:01:52,348 --> 00:01:53,689
A red scarf, bag

43
00:01:53,739 --> 00:01:56,037
or bracelet keeps it close.

44
00:01:56,087 --> 00:01:58,298
Let it remind you to smile

45
00:01:58,348 --> 00:01:59,950
and stay confident.

46
00:02:00,000 --> 00:02:02,734
Lucky direction: the south.

47
00:02:02,784 --> 00:02:06,136
Best time: from 11 in the morning

48
00:02:06,186 --> 00:02:08,404
to 1 in the afternoon.

49
00:02:08,454 --> 00:02:09,950
Plan around it.

50
00:02:10,000 --> 00:02:14,437

## Keep the voice the same in every scene
Flow makes the voice fresh for every clip, so the voice text in every scene is written to describe exactly the same voice: the same words, in the same order, at the start of the audio line, for every scene and every video. To keep it that way:
- Always use "Copy + English voice" or "Copy + Khmer voice". Do not retype, shorten or translate the voice text, and do not add other words about the voice.
- Make all clips of one video in the same Flow project, with the same model and settings for every clip.
- Use the same language for the whole video (all English or all Khmer).
- Listen to each clip as it is made. If one sounds different, make only that clip again with the same copied prompt.

## Khmer voice – how to get clear Khmer
Every Khmer voice line in this pack is written for speaking: short sentences, a ។ at each pause, and numbers written out in Khmer words (for example ហុកសិបបួន, not 64) so the voice does not read them in English. Subtitles and captions keep the digits.

### If you use Google Flow
Use "Copy + Khmer voice" for each scene; the voice description is already included. Flow's Khmer can be unclear on some clips. Listen to every clip, and remake any clip where words are wrong, cut off or English-sounding. If Khmer stays unclear, make the clips without voice ("Copy") and add a recorded Khmer voice-over instead.

### If you use a text-to-speech app
- Language: Cambodian Khmer (km-KH). Pick a real Khmer voice, for example Microsoft Azure's female voice "km-KH-SreymomNeural" (used by many TTS apps and sites). Do not use an English voice with Khmer text.
- Speed: 0.85× to 0.9×. Pitch: natural. Pause between sentences: normal.
- Paste the Khmer voice-over text from this pack exactly as it is (Khmer script, no romanization).
- Make one scene at a time if the app cuts off long text, then join the clips in your editor.
- Before using a new voice, test it with these two sentences and listen carefully:
  សួស្តីអ្នកទាំងអស់គ្នា! សូមស្វាគមន៍មកកាន់ MyBirthSign.com។
  ថ្ងៃនេះជាថ្ងៃល្អសម្រាប់អ្នកកើតឆ្នាំខ្លា។ សូមជឿជាក់លើខ្លួនឯង ហើយកុំភ្លេចអត់ធ្មត់។
  Check that every word is clear Cambodian Khmer, sentence endings are complete, and speed and volume stay even.

### Record it yourself
A native speaker reading the Khmer voice-over slowly and clearly is always the most natural choice.

## Style (paste first in every scene)
Vertical 9:16. Photorealistic live-action fantasy film, the look of a high-budget fantasy epic that blends Khmer royal heritage with the grandeur of a Chinese historical fantasy drama. Real human faces with natural skin texture, the zodiac characters saved in Flow, each named after its animal (@Rat, @Ox, @Tiger, @Rabbit, @Dragon, @Snake, @Horse, @Goat, @Monkey, @Rooster, @Dog, @Pig): a guardian warrior in ornate armor themed on the animal, with a lifelike animal beside them (realistic anatomy, real fur, feathers or scales), always exactly as in the saved character picture, noble and powerful (never cute toys or cartoons). Setting: a majestic Khmer temple inspired by Angkor Wat with towering carved stone, an enormous luminous full moon, deep indigo starry sky, drifting mist, a still reflecting pool with floating pink lotus flowers and ornate golden lanterns. A living, magical sky in every shot: thin silver clouds drift slowly across the moon, stars twinkle, a soft halo pulses around the moon and sky lanterns rise gently in the distance. Action and magic in the style of a Chinese fantasy (xianxia) drama: graceful wire-fu flying leaps, slow-motion martial-arts moves, flowing robes and capes, glowing sword energy and swirling qi light, always without blood, injury or fighting between characters. Golden magic: shimmering gold particles, flowing ribbons of liquid-gold light and floating gold dust that never hide a face. Physically real lighting: cool silver-blue moonlight, warm golden rim light, soft lantern glow, volumetric light rays; colors deep navy, imperial gold, ruby red and lotus pink. Cinematic depth of field, smooth slow camera movement, high-end visual effects, 4K detail. The zodiac guardians and their animals never talk and never move their mouths; only Chanda, the presenter, speaks. No text, no letters, no subtitles, no watermark, no logos, no cartoon or plastic look, no low-quality CGI.

For each scene, add Chanda and the characters the prompt names as ingredients; the prompts below point to them with @ (for example @Snake), so keep the names in Flow exactly the same.

### Character reference image prompts
- **Chanda (ចន្ទា), the presenter (make once, use in every video):** Create a photorealistic portrait from a high-budget fantasy epic that blends Khmer royal heritage with a Chinese historical fantasy drama: @Chanda, a beautiful Cambodian woman in her early twenties with a warm, kind and confident presence: oval face, soft defined cheekbones, warm amber-brown almond eyes, natural brows, rosy cheeks, natural skin with real texture and refined natural makeup, glossy black hair swept into a low side bun on her right side held by an elaborate golden lotus hairpin with fine dangling gold chains, delicate gold flower earrings, a fitted cream-and-gold Khmer silk brocade blouse with a high mandarin collar and long sleeves, a large round gold medallion on a chain engraved with all twelve zodiac animals, an ornate gold belt with a single ruby-red gemstone at the buckle, a deep navy silk sampot to the ankles with a vertical gold-embroidered front panel and a wide band of Khmer gold patterns at the hem, and gold bangles on both wrists. Realistic human proportions, natural facial expression with a gentle closed-lip smile, real silk that folds and catches the light, real gold jewelry with true metallic reflections. Graceful, upright posture, one hand held open toward the temple as if presenting it, the other resting at her waist. She stands barefoot on a carved sandstone temple terrace with an ornate stone balustrade, beside a reflecting pool with pink lotus flowers; behind her, a temple inspired by Angkor Wat glows under an enormous luminous full moon and a deep indigo starry sky, with golden lanterns and soft drifting gold dust. ## Scene 1 – Dog day (Chanda on camera)
@Chanda, a beautiful Cambodian woman in her early twenties with a warm, kind and confident presence: oval face, soft defined cheekbones, warm amber-brown almond eyes, natural brows, rosy cheeks, natural skin with real texture and refined natural makeup, glossy black hair swept into a low side bun on her right side held by an elaborate golden lotus hairpin with fine dangling gold chains, delicate gold flower earrings, a fitted cream-and-gold Khmer silk brocade blouse with a high mandarin collar and long sleeves, a large round gold medallion on a chain engraved with all twelve zodiac animals, an ornate gold belt with a single ruby-red gemstone at the buckle, a deep navy silk sampot to the ankles with a vertical gold-embroidered front panel and a wide band of Khmer gold patterns at the hem, and gold bangles on both wrists, stands in front of the Angkor-style temple at night, facing the camera and speaking to the viewer with natural lip movements, then turns and gestures toward the pond. Her face is full of genuine excitement, as if sharing wonderful news with a close friend: her eyes light up, her eyebrows lift and a bright, natural smile spreads across her face as she leans slightly toward the camera. Natural, believable acting with real micro-expressions, never exaggerated or theatrical. Chanda is the saved Flow character @Chanda. Then: magical and powerful. @Dog (the Dog guardian with their dog) stands on its glowing lotus pedestal on the pond in front of the Angkor-style temple under a huge full moon. Its eyes light up, a golden aura bursts around it, and it leaps through the air to catch a glowing golden disc thrown by its guardian, who spins and catches it back in one smooth slow-motion move, as today's Metal magic swirls around them: bright silver-white sparkles, shimmering blades of light and softly chiming silver stars. The lotus pond ripples with golden light, and lanterns rise into the sky. Dynamic camera: start close on its face, then sweep up and follow it through the air. Cute, majestic and full of energy, matching the reference image.

## Scene 2 – Lucky signs
Magical and powerful. @Dog (the Dog guardian with their dog), @Tiger (the Tiger guardian with their tiger) and @Horse (the Horse guardian with their horse) fly in from three directions on trails of golden light and land together on glowing lotus pedestals in front of the temple. As they land, a ring of golden energy explodes outward, lotus flowers bloom across the pond and a shower of sparkles and gold coins falls from the sky. They look at each other and cheer. The camera orbits around them in a smooth circle. The animals match the reference images.

## Scene 3 – Dragon, take it easy
Photorealistic golden fantasy-epic style, same saved Flow characters (Chanda and the zodiac characters named after their animals). @Dragon (the Dragon guardian with their dragon) rests on a lotus pedestal by the pond: it curls up under a carved stone arch as soft golden light glows around it, and the full moon shines calmly on the still water. ## Scene 4 – Outro
Magical and powerful. The whole lotus pond lights up from below, and its golden glow rises into the sky like a curtain of light, and the huge full moon glows over the pond. The camera rises up and pulls back for a wide, epic view, leaving open sky at the top of the frame for a title.
